<script type="text/javascript">
	head.ready('jquery_nyroModal', function() {
		$(document).ready(function() {
			$("#search-input").autocomplete({
				source: $("#search-input").data('ajax-url'),
				minLength: 2,
				select: function( event, ui ) {
					window.location.href = ui.item.description.title;
				}
			}).data( "autocomplete" )._renderItem = function( ul, item ) {
			

				if (item.category == 'categoryHeader') {
					return $("<li></li>").data( "item.autocomplete", item).append('<div class="ac-headline clearfix"><h3>' + item.label + '</h3></div>' ).appendTo(ul);
				}
				
				if (item.category == 'category') {
				
						var isMiele = "";
						var imgProp = 'height="17" width="21"';
						
					if(item.description.title == "/miele.html") {
						isMiele = "miele"
						item.image = "/public/a5/ee/73/104012_212126_miele_logo_small.png";
						imgProp = 'height="35" width="100"';
					}
				
				
				return $('<li class="category '+isMiele+'"></li>').data( "item.autocomplete", item).append('<a href="' + item.description.title + '" class="ac-item ' + item.cls + '"><div class="ac-img-holder"><img class="ac-item-image" '+imgProp+' src="' + item.image + '" /></div><div class="ac-headline ">' + item.label + '</div></a>' ).appendTo(ul);
				}
				
				if (item.category == 'commodity') {
					return $('<li class="commodity"></li>').data( "item.autocomplete", item).append('<a href="' + item.description.title + '" class="ac-item ' + item.cls + '"><div class="ac-img-holder"><img class="ac-item-image" height="" src="' + item.image + '" /></div><div class="ac-headline">' + item.label + '</div><div class="ac-description" title="' + item.description.title + '">' + item.description.text + "</div></a>" ).appendTo(ul);
				}
				if (item.category == 'delimiter') {
					return $("<li></li>").data( "item.autocomplete", item).append('<div class="ac-delimiter ' + item.cls + '"></div><div class="ac-headline"><h3>' + item.label + '</h3></div>').appendTo(ul);
				}
				
			};
		});
	});
</script>

<script>
	var langs = 'en,vi,ru,hu';
	var gtrans = {
		getLang : function(){
			var C = document.cookie.split(';');
			var d = 'default'
	    for(var i=0; i<C.length; i++) {
        var c = C[i];
        while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1);
        if (c.indexOf('googtrans=') == 0) return c.substring('googtrans='.length,c.length).replace('/sk/','') || d;
	    }
	    return d;
		},
		setLang : function(lang){
			lang = lang || 'default';
			var d = new Date( Date.now()+100*60*60*24*30*6 ); 
			document.cookie="googtrans=; expires=-1; path=/; domain=.datart.sk";
			document.cookie="googtrans=; expires=-1; path=/";
			if( lang != 'default' && langs.indexOf(lang) > -1 ){
				document.cookie="googtrans=/sk/"+lang+"; expires="+d.toUTCString()+"; path=/; domain=.datart.sk";
				document.cookie="googtrans=/sk/"+lang+"; expires="+d.toUTCString()+"; path=/";
			}
		},
		changeFlag : function(){

			setInterval(function() {
      	document.getElementById('gtrans-current-flag').className = "css-flag css-flag-"+gtrans.getLang();
      }, 1000);
		},
		init : function(){
			new google.translate.TranslateElement({pageLanguage: 'sk',includedLanguages: langs, layout: google.translate.TranslateElement.FloatPosition.TOP_LEFT, autoDisplay: false, multilanguagePage: true}, 'gtransELEM');
			gtrans.changeFlag();
		},
		load : function(){
			var head = document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
			var script = document.createElement('script');
			script.src = "//translate.google.com/translate_a/element.js?cb=gtrans.init";
			head.appendChild(script);
		}
		
	}
	

	
    if (gtrans.getLang() !== 'default') {
        if( typeof head != 'undefined' ){
            head.ready( gtrans.load );
        }else{
            gtrans.load();
        }
    }
</script></div>

<script type="text/javascript">
	head.ready(function(){
		$(document).ready(function() {
			$('.muj-datart').click(function(event){
				event.preventDefault();
			});
			var $login = $('#registration-login');
			$("#registration-login").menuAim({
				activate: function(row) {
					$login.addClass('active');	
				},
				deactivate: function(row) {
				var isHovered = !!$('.tooltipster-base').
                    filter(function() { return $(this).is(":hover"); }).length;
                    
					if (!$("#registration-login input").is(":focus") || isHovered) {
						$login.removeClass('active');
					}
				},
				exitMenu: function() {
					var isHovered = !!$('.tooltipster-base').
                    filter(function() { return $(this).is(":hover"); }).length;		

					if(isHovered){
						return false;
					}
					else{
						return true;
					}
				},
				submenuDirection: "below",
				tolerance: 75
			});
			$('#registration-login .tooltiper, #userinfo-mob-modal .tooltiper').tooltip({
			   trigger: 'hover',
			   contentAsHTML: true,
			   interactive: true
			});
		});
	});
</script>

			head.ready('jquery_nyroModal', function(){
				$(document).ready(function() {
					/* accordeon u mensich rozliseni  */
					//var windowWidth = window.outerWidth;
					var windowWidth = $(window).width();
					
					var footerAccordeon = function() {
						$('.dt-sites h2').unbind( "click" );
						if(windowWidth < 992) {
							$('.dt-sites ul').hide();
							$('.dt-sites h2').click(function(){
								if($(this).hasClass('active')) {
									$(this).removeClass('active').siblings('ul').hide();
								} else {
									$(this).addClass('active').siblings('ul').show();
								}
								
							});
						} else {
							$('.dt-sites ul').show();
						}
					}

					footerAccordeon();

					$(window).resize(function() {
						windowWidth = window.outerWidth;
						footerAccordeon();
					});
				});
			});
		</script>

<!-- CLICKABLE SIDE JS CORE -->
<script type="text/javascript">

	head.ready(function () {

	function ClickableSides(sideActivate) {
		
		this.self = this;
		this.sideActivate = sideActivate;
		this.minWindowWidth = 930;
		this.leftSide = $('#clickableLeftSide');
		this.rightSide = $('#clickableRightSide');
		
		this.calculateVariables = function() {
			
			this.windowWidth = $(window).width();
			this.documentWidth = $(document).width();
			this.documentHeight = $(document).height();
			this.pageWrapperWidth = $(".page-wrapper").width();
			this.sideBoxWidth = (this.documentWidth-this.pageWrapperWidth)/2;
			this.headerHeight = $("#header-wrapper").height() + parseInt($("#header-wrapper").css("margin-bottom")) + $("#info-top-bar").height() + 50;
			this.footerHeight = $("#footer-real-wrapper").height();
			
			//console.log("inside variables recalculated");
		
		}
		
		this.calculateSideHeight = function() {
			var sideHeight = this.documentHeight-this.headerHeight-this.footerHeight;
			return sideHeight;
		}
		
		this.resizeLeftSide = function(jqElement) {
			this.leftSide.css({"width" : this.sideBoxWidth+"px",
										 		 "height" : this.calculateSideHeight()+"px",
										 		 "left" : "-"+this.sideBoxWidth+"px"});
			//console.log("left side resized");
		}
		
		this.resizeRightSide = function(jqElement) {
			this.rightSide.css({"width" : this.sideBoxWidth+"px",
										 		 "height" : this.calculateSideHeight()+"px",
										 		 "right" : "-"+this.sideBoxWidth+"px"});
										 		 
			//console.log("right side resized");
		}
		
		this.resizeSides = function() {
		
				this.calculateVariables();
			
				switch (this.sideActivate) {
			
					case "all": 
						this.resizeLeftSide();
						this.resizeRightSide();
						break;
					
					case "left":
						this.rightSide.hide();
						this.resizeLeftSide();
						break;
						
					case "right": 
						this.leftSide.hide();
						this.resizeRightSide();
						break;
			
				}

		}
		
		this.standByMode = function() {
		
			if( $(window).width() > this.minWindowWidth) {
				return false;
			}
			else {
				//console.log("stand-by mode activated");
				return true;
			}
			
		}
		
		this.targetedPage = function() {
		
		}
		
		
		if(this.standByMode() === false) {
			this.resizeSides();
		}
		
		
	}
	
	 var sidesClicks = new ClickableSides("all");
	 
	 $(window).resize(function() {
	 
	 		if(sidesClicks.standByMode() === false) {
				sidesClicks.resizeSides();
			}
			
		});

	
	});

</script>

<script>
				
					if( ! sessionStorage.getItem('welcomeCount') ){
						sessionStorage.setItem('welcomeCount', 0);
					}
			</script>
<script>
				head.ready(['jquery', 'jquery_nyroModal'], function() {	
					$(document).on('click', function(){
						if(sessionStorage.getItem('welcomeCount')){
							var count = parseInt( sessionStorage.getItem('welcomeCount') );
							count ++;
							sessionStorage.setItem('welcomeCount', count );
						}
						if(sessionStorage.getItem('disableWelcome') ){
								sessionStorage.removeItem('welcomeCount');
						}		
						if( ! sessionStorage.getItem('disableWelcome') ){
							if( count >= 3 ){
								setTimeout( 
									function(){		
										$('#WelcomeCampaignLink').nyroModal();
										sessionStorage.setItem('disableWelcome', true );
										$('#WelcomeCampaignLink').click();
									},
									1500
								)
							}							
						}
					});
					
					if( ! sessionStorage.getItem('disableWelcome') ){
						if( parseInt(sessionStorage.getItem('welcomeCount')) >= 3 ){
							sessionStorage.setItem('disableWelcome', true );
							setTimeout( 
								function(){		
									$('#WelcomeCampaignLink').nyroModal();
									$('#WelcomeCampaignLink').click();
								},
								1500
							)
						}							
					}
				})
			</script>
